<style id='wp-emoji-styles-inline-css'>

	img.wp-smiley, img.emoji {
		display: inline !important;
		border: none !important;
		box-shadow: none !important;
		height: 1em !important;
		width: 1em !important;
		margin: 0 0.07em !important;
		vertical-align: -0.1em !important;
		background: none !important;
		padding: 0 !important;
	}
</style>

<!-- start Simple Custom CSS and JS -->
<script>
jQuery(document).ready(function( $ ){
	if (window.location.href === 'https://nuflow.com/?p=18099') {
		var $sidebar = $('#sidebar-main');
		var $cta = $('#plumbing-problems-cta');

		if ($sidebar.length && $cta.length) {
			var originalOffset = $sidebar.position().top; // Compatível com jQuery 1.x

			$(window).scroll(function() {
				var scrollTop = $(window).scrollTop();
				var ctaOffset = $cta.position().top; // Compatível com jQuery 1.x
				var sidebarHeight = $sidebar.outerHeight();
				var stopPosition = ctaOffset - sidebarHeight;

				if (scrollTop >= originalOffset && scrollTop < stopPosition) {
					$sidebar.css({
						position: 'fixed',
						top: '0'
					});
				} else if (scrollTop >= stopPosition) {
					$sidebar.css({
						position: 'absolute',
						top: stopPosition - originalOffset + 'px'
					});
				} else {
					$sidebar.css({
						position: 'static',
						top: 'auto'
					});
				}
			});
		}
	}
});</script>

	display: block;
	width: 17px;
	height: auto;
}
header #ast-desktop-header .site-above-header-wrap {
	max-width: 100% !important;
}

#ast-hf-menu-2 .ast-icon.icon-arrow{
	display: none !important;
}

#menu-item-5711 img{
	width: 22px !important;
}

#ast-hf-menu-5 img{
  width: 16px;
  margin-right: 2px;
}

#ast-hf-menu-5 a{
  font-weight: 800;
  font-family: 'Montserrat';
}

#menu-item-730 img{
  width: 20px;
}

#ast-mobile-header .ast-above-header-wrap{
  z-index: 9999;
  position: relative;
}

#menu_5-site-navigation-mobile img{
	margin-left: 0 !important;
}

#menu-item-729{
	width: 85%;
}

#menu-item-730{
	padding-right: 10px;
}

#menu_5-site-navigation-mobile a{
	font-size: 12px;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

header .ast-builder-grid-row.ast-grid-center-col-layout {
  grid-template-columns: 1fr auto 1fr;
}

/*Hide main blog*/
.nuflow-single-blog h1 {
	font-family: Montserrat;
	font-weight: 700;
	font-size: 3.3em;
}
.nuflow-single-blog h2, .nuflow-single-blog h3, .nuflow-single-blog h4, .nuflow-single-blog h5 {
	font-family: Montserrat;
}
.nuflow-single-blog .fl-accordion-button .fl-accordion-button-label {
	font-weight: 600 !important;
}
.fl-accordion-item-active .fl-accordion-button-label, .fl-accordion-item-active .fl-accordion-button-icon {
		border-bottom: none !important;
}
.fl-accordion-button-icon {
	color: #549C44;
	opacity: 1;
}

.nuflow-single-blog .entry-meta{
	margin-bottom: 25px !important;
}

.nuflow-single-blog .entry-meta,
.nuflow-single-blog .entry-meta a{
	font-family: Montserrat;
	color: #091a31 !important;
	font-size: 17px;
}

.nuflow-single-blog .post-thumb-img-content img{
	max-height: 550px;
object-fit: cover;
}

/*Adjust Pressure Pipe Lining page*/
.content-text li{
	font-size: 17px;
	line-height: 1.4em;
}

.page-pressure-pipe-lining .mfp-iframe-scaler iframe.mfp-iframe{
	width: 100%;
	height: 100%;
	top: 0;
	left: 0;
}

.page-pressure-pipe-lining .mfp-wrap.fl-video-lightbox-wrap .mfp-close{
	color: #fff !important;
}

.page-pressure-pipe-lining .section-asoe .fl-row-content-wrap .col-asoe .title-asoe h1 strong{
	position: relative;
}

.page-pressure-pipe-lining .section-asoe .fl-row-content-wrap .col-asoe .title-asoe h1 strong:after{
	position: relative;
	width: 130px;
	height: 40px;
	background-size: contain;
	background-position: center;
	transform: none;
	display: inline-block;
	left: 5px;
	right: auto;
	vertical-align: middle;
	margin-bottom: 5px;
}		

/*Sticky Sidebar*/
html, body{
	overflow: initial;
}

.sidebar-main{
	position: sticky;
	top: 150px;
}

@media (max-width: 921px){
	.sidebar-main{
		display: none;
	}
}

/*Misc adjustments to testimonials section*/
.section-used-in.section-2 .fl-col-content,
.section-used-in.section-3 .fl-col-content{
	padding-top: 0;
}

/*Adjust Nuflow logo in "Grow Your Business page"*/
.grow-your-page .flex-desktop img{
	width: 260px;
	height: 100% !important;
	object-position: bottom;
	margin-left: 10px;
}

/*Fix dropdown menu issue on mobile*/
.ast-menu-toggle{
	width: 30px !important;
  height: 100% !important;
}

.ast-menu-toggle .ast-icon::before{
	top: 0;
}

div.uabb-blog-posts-col-1:nth-child(1) > div:nth-child(1) > div:nth-child(1) > div:nth-child(1) > a:nth-child(1) > img:nth-child(1) {
	object-fit: contain;
}
.nuflow-single-blog .container-single-blog article#post-18807 header .post-thumb-img-content img {
	object-fit: contain !important;
	max-height: 180px !important;
}

.image-previous-events img {
	height: 300px;
	object-fit: cover;
}

/*Fix font size*/
.nuflow-single-blog .container-single-blog article.type-post .entry-content ul li {
  font-family: Montserrat !important;
  font-weight: 400;
  font-size: 18px;
  line-height: 38px;
  color: #6c6e81;
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .nuflow-single-blog .container-single-blog article.type-post .entry-content ul li{
    font-size: 16px;
    line-height: 1.6em;
  }
}

/*Misc post updates adjustments*/
.fl-col-content{
	display: block;
}

.fl-button{
	background-color: #549c44 !important;
	border: 1px solid #489038 !important;
}

.fl-button:hover{
	background-color: #3F842F !important;
}

#iconsGreen img {
	max-width: 100px;
}		</style>

        sewer backups</h2>
                                                                                        <h2>

<div class="fl-module fl-module-rich-text fl-node-62f504c7630cf col-left-content" data-node="62f504c7630cf">
	<div class="fl-module-content fl-node-content">
		<div class="fl-rich-text">
	<p>NuFlow rehabilitates old, broken, blocked or corroded pipes using pipe lining technologies. Pipe lining creates a new pipe inside the old one, and the result is a seamless “pipe-within-a-pipe” that solves backup problems, seals cracks, and prevents further corrosion and root intrusion.</p>
</div>

<div class="fl-col-group fl-node-62f504c7630ef" data-node="62f504c7630ef">
			<div class="fl-col fl-node-62f504c7630f0 fl-col-bg-color" data-node="62f504c7630f0">
	<div class="fl-col-content fl-node-content"><div class="fl-module fl-module-rich-text fl-node-62f504c7630f1 nuflow-content" data-node="62f504c7630f1">
	<div class="fl-module-content fl-node-content">
		<div class="fl-rich-text">
	<p>Old, broken, blocked or corroded water and sewer/drain pipes are problems often compounded by root intrusion, scale build up, or build up from fats, oils and grease. The pipe lining process is non-invasive, fast, and reliable, and because it uses existing plumbing access points like cleanouts and toilet openings there is little if any damage to the property that needs to be repaired. There’s no need to tear out walls or cut up slabs!</p>
</div>
	</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
	</div>
		</div>
	</div>
</div>

<div class="fl-col-group fl-node-62f504c7630fa" data-node="62f504c7630fa">
			<div class="fl-col fl-node-62f504c7630fb fl-col-bg-color less-hassle-container" data-node="62f504c7630fb">
	<div class="fl-col-content fl-node-content"><div class="fl-module fl-module-heading fl-node-62f504c763164 repair-title" data-node="62f504c763164">
	<div class="fl-module-content fl-node-content">
		<h2 class="fl-heading">
		<span class="fl-heading-text">Why Choose Pipe Lining?</span>
	</h2>
	</div>
</div>
<div class="fl-module fl-module-html fl-node-62f504c7630fc" data-node="62f504c7630fc">
	<div class="fl-module-content fl-node-content">
		<div class="fl-html">
	        <div class="less-hassle-home">
            <div class="less-hassle-home-nav">
                                                            <div class="less-hassle-home-nav-item active"><h2>Less Hassle</h2></div>
                                        <div class="less-hassle-home-nav-content-mobile">
                        <div class="container-content-mobile">
                            <div class="nav-title"><h3>NuFlow Makes it Easy</h3></div>
                            <div class="nav-desc">
                                <p>
        The pipe lining process utilizes existing plumbing system access points
        like drain cleanouts and toilets, so we don’t need to trench the yard or tear out walls and ceilings.
        Old fashioned pipe replacement is expensive, messy, and time consuming.</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                                                            <div class="less-hassle-home-nav-item"><h2>Costs Less</h2></div>
                                        <div class="less-hassle-home-nav-content-mobile">
                        <div class="container-content-mobile">
                            <div class="nav-title"><h3>NuFlow Makes it Easy</h3></div>
                            <div class="nav-desc">
                                <p>
        Trenching landscape and cutting up (and repairing) walls and ceilings typically
        makes pipe replacement more expensive. Repairing pipes using existing access points almost
        always costs less than replacement.</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                                                            <div class="less-hassle-home-nav-item"><h2>WORKS ON ANY Pipe</h2></div>
                                        <div class="less-hassle-home-nav-content-mobile">
                        <div class="container-content-mobile">
                            <div class="nav-title"><h3>NuFlow Makes it Easy</h3></div>
                            <div class="nav-desc">
                                <p>
        Roof and storm drains, in-building drain and water pipes, sewer laterals and mains, vertical drain stacks, sanitary drain, waste and vent systems can all be lined with NuFlow pipe lining technologies.
    </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                            </div>
            <div class="less-hassle-home-content">
                <div class="less-hassle-home-content-box">
                    <div class="box-title"><h3>NuFlow Makes it Easy</h3></div>
                    <div class="container-content">
                                                                                    <div class="box-desc show">
                                    <p>
        The pipe lining process utilizes existing plumbing system access points
        like drain cleanouts and toilets, so we don’t need to trench the yard or tear out walls and ceilings.
        Old fashioned pipe replacement is expensive, messy, and time consuming.</p>
                                </div>
                                                                                        <div class="box-desc">
                                    <p>
        Trenching landscape and cutting up (and repairing) walls and ceilings typically
        makes pipe replacement more expensive. Repairing pipes using existing access points almost
        always costs less than replacement.</p>
                                </div>
                                                                                    <div class="box-desc">
                                    <p>
        Roof and storm drains, in-building drain and water pipes, sewer laterals and mains, vertical drain stacks, sanitary drain, waste and vent systems can all be lined with NuFlow pipe lining technologies.
    </p>
                                </div>
                                            </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        </div>
	</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
	</div>

<div class="fl-col-group fl-node-62f504c7630fd" data-node="62f504c7630fd">
			<div class="fl-col fl-node-62f504c7630fe fl-col-bg-color fl-col-has-cols nudrain-nuflow-nuline" data-node="62f504c7630fe">

	<div class="review-slider-wrapper">
            <div class="review-card">
            <div class="card-title">
                <h3>Peerless Plumbing</h3>
            </div>
            <div class="card-content">
                <p>The turn-around time and method of doing trenchless work save time, money, headache, and create a better end result!</p>
            </div>
            <div class="card-ratings">
                                    <span class="star"></span>
                                                        <span class="star"></span>

                    <img decoding="async" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Gary stern" data-lazy-src="/wp-content/uploads/2021/10/Gary-Stern.png"><noscript><img decoding="async" src="/wp-content/uploads/2021/10/Gary-Stern.png" alt="Gary stern"></noscript>

            <div class="review-card">
            <div class="card-title">
                <h3>Lomonaco Coast Plumbing</h3>
            </div>
            <div class="card-content">
                <p>After hiring 3 separate plumbers to find the issue, Mike was able to both find and repair the main sewer line without tearing up my front patio and lawn.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="card-ratings">
                                    <span class="star"></span>
                                                        <span class="star"></span>
                                                        <span class="star"></span>
                                                        <span class="star"></span>
                                                        <span class="star"></span>
                                                </div>

<!--[if lt IE 9]><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://nuflow.com/wp-content/plugins/gallery-lightbox-slider '/css/photobox/photobox.ie.css'.'"><![endif]-->

<style type="text/css">
#pbOverlay { background:rgba(0,0,0,.90)  none repeat scroll 0% 0% !important; }
	.gallery-caption, .blocks-gallery-item figcaption {}	.pbWrapper > img{display: inline;}
	#pbThumbsToggler {display: none !important;}
</style>

<script type="text/javascript">// <![CDATA[
jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
	/* START --- Gallery Lightbox Lite --- */
	
	/* Replace default title to more fancy :) */
	$('.gallery img').each(function(i) {

		$alt = $(this).attr('alt');

		$(this).attr('alt', $alt.replace(/-|_/g, ' '));

		$altnew = $(this).attr('alt').replace(/\b[a-z]/g, function(letter) {

			    return letter.toUpperCase();

			});

		$(this).attr('alt', $altnew );

	});

		
    function escapeHtml(unsafe) {
        return unsafe
            .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
            .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
            .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
            .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
            .replace(/'/g, "&#039;");
    }
	/* Gutenberg Adaptive */
	$('.blocks-gallery-item, .wp-block-image').each(function(i) {

		var $blck = $(this).find('img'),
		$isSrc = $blck.attr('src');

		if (! $blck.closest('a').length) {
			$blck.wrap('<a class="glg-a-custom-wrap" href="'+$isSrc+'"></a>');
		}
		else {
			$blck.closest('a').addClass('glg-a-custom-wrap');
		}

        /* Sanitize */
        if (typeof $blck.attr('alt') !== 'undefined' && $blck.attr('alt') !== '') {
            $blck.attr('alt', escapeHtml($blck.attr('alt')));
        }

	});

	
	/* Initialize!
	.glg-a-custom-wrap (Block Gallery)
	.carousel-item:not(".bx-clone") > a:not(".icp_custom_link") (Image Carousel)
	.gallery-item > dt > a (Native Gallery) */
	$('.gallery, .ghozylab-gallery, .wp-block-gallery')
		.photobox('.carousel-item > a:not(".icp_custom_link"),a.glg-a-custom-wrap, .gallery-item > dt > a, .gallery-item > div > a',{
			autoplay: false,

<script>
function jumptosection (target) {
	var offset_target = jQuery('#'+target).offset();
	var scrolltopfix = offset_target.top-jQuery('#masthead').height();
	if (typeof jQuery('#wpadminbar').height() !== 'undefined') {
		scrolltopfix = scrolltopfix - jQuery('#wpadminbar').height();
	}
	jQuery(document).scrollTop(scrolltopfix);
}</script>
